<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B7872B8A-9943-4EDF-A994-E7E5E984DD66'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A teacher shares a large packet of sweets evenly to her class of $24$ pupils and then eats the left over sweets.
There were $170$ sweets in the packet, how many does the teacher eat?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text{Sweets per pupil}     &= \dfrac {170} {24} \\\\
                            &= 7 \text{ remainder } 2 \\\\
                            &\approx 7 \\\\
\text{Sweets left}          &= \text{Total sweets} - \text{Sweets distributed to pupils} \\\\
                            &= 170 - 24 \times 7 \\\\
                            &= 170 - 168 \\\\
                            &= 2
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text{Sweets per pupil}     &= \dfrac {170} {24} \\\\
                            &= 7 \text{ remainder } 2 \\\\
                            &\approx 7 \\\\
\text{Sweets left}          &= \text{Total sweets} - \text{Sweets distributed to pupils} \\\\
                            &= 170 - 24 \times 7 \\\\
                            &= 170 - 168 \\\\
                            &= 2
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-019418D0-2051-4766-9C4C-2B49BE77FB1B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Jordan has a collection of $1345$ marbles! He gives his friend $689$ of the marbles. How many does Jordan have left?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Marbles left}              &= \text{Total marbles} - \text{Marbles given to friend} \\\\
                                  &= 1345 - 689 \\\\
                                  &= 656
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$656$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Marbles left}              &= \text{Total marbles} - \text{Marbles given to friend} \\\\
                                  &= 1345 - 689 \\\\
                                  &= 656
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$656$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0AC56C7D-FC28-4B99-925E-27D79107E4CE'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Alison, Bhavini and Catherine share a bag of sweets.
Alison gets $5$ more sweets than Bhavini and Catherine gets twice as many sweets as Alison.

Catherine gets $22$ sweets. How many sweets are there in the bag?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Catherine get}   &= 22 \ \text {sweets} \\\\
                        &= 2 \times \text {Alison} \\\\
\text {Alison get}      &= \dfrac {22} {2} \\\\
                        &= \text {Bhavini} + 5 \\\\
\text {Bhavini get}     &= \text {Alison} - 5 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {22} {2} - 5
\end {aligned}
$

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Total sweets}    &= \dfrac {22} {2} + \dfrac {22} {2} - 5 + 22 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 11} {2} + \dfrac {2 \times 11} {2} - 5 + 22 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 11} {\cancel {2}} + \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 11} {\cancel {2}} - 5 + 22 \\\\
                        &= 11 + 11 - 5 + 22 \\\\
                        &= 44 - 5 \\\\
                        &= 39
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$39$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Catherine get}   &= 22 \ \text {sweets} \\\\
                        &= 2 \times \text {Alison} \\\\
\text {Alison get}      &= \dfrac {22} {2} \\\\
                        &= \text {Bhavini} + 5 \\\\
\text {Bhavini get}     &= \text {Alison} - 5 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {22} {2} - 5
\end {aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Total sweets}    &= \dfrac {22} {2} + \dfrac {22} {2} - 5 + 22 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 11} {2} + \dfrac {2 \times 11} {2} - 5 + 22 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 11} {\cancel {2}} + \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 11} {\cancel {2}} - 5 + 22 \\\\
                        &= 11 + 11 - 5 + 22 \\\\
                        &= 44 - 5 \\\\
                        &= 39
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$39$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

$25\%$ of $48 = {?}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 25 \% \ \text {of} \ 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 48} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 4 \times 12} {25 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {25} \times \cancel {4} \times 12} {\cancel {25} \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

$25\%$ of ${?} = 48$

<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
25 \% \ \text{of} \ a       &= 48 \\\\
\dfrac {25} {100} \times a  &= 48 \\\\
a                           &= 48 \times \dfrac {100} {25} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {48 \times 100} {25} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {48 \times 4 \times 25} {25} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {48 \times 4 \times \cancel {25}} {\cancel {25}} \\\\
a                           &= 48 \times 4 \\\\
a                           &= 192
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the two square numbers $= a, b$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}

\end{aligned}

We can easily infer the value using trial and error.

| Value of $a$ | Value of $b$ |       Sum        |

|     $16$     |     $49$     |  $16 + 49 = 65$  |
|     $25$     |     $49$     |  $25 + 49 = 74$  |

Two square numbers $= 25, 49$

Identify the two **prime** numbers that add up to 13

${?} + {?} = 13$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the two square numbers $= a, b$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + b       &= 13
\end{aligned}
$

We can easily infer the value using trial and error.

| Value of $a$ | Value of $b$ |       Sum        |
|:------------:|:------------:|:----------------:|
|     $2$      |     $3$      |   $2 + 3 = 5$    |
|     $2$      |     $5$      |   $2 + 5 = 7$    |
|     $2$      |     $7$      |   $2 + 7 = 9$    |
|     $2$      |     $11$     |  $2 + 11 = 13$   |

Two square numbers $= 2, 11$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2, 11$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

|     $2$      |     $11$     |  $2 + 11 = 13$   |
<emptyline>
Two square numbers $= 2, 11$
</code></pre>

<div class='question question'>

**For question 1 to 3 use this information:**

**A box contains $\bold{54}$ biscuits of which $\bold{16}$ are chocolate**.

How many biscuits are not chocolate?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Not chocolate}   &= 54 - 16  \\\\
           &= 38
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$38$

</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0A3E5DD3-7A11-483C-A187-0DA57B1DD81B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many people can have four biscuits each?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {The number of people that can have four biscuit}     &= \dfrac {54} {4} \\\\
                                                            &= 13 \text{ remainder } 2 \\\\
                                                            &\approx 13
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {The number of people that can have four biscuit}     &= \dfrac {54} {4} \\\\
                                                            &= 13 \text{ remainder } 2 \\\\
                                                            &\approx 13
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$13$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6053AC7E-A234-4868-BF46-DDF058218EC1'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many biscuits are there in seven boxes?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total biscuits}   &= \text{Number of boxes} \times \text{Number of biscuits in each box} \\\\
                        &= 7 \times 54 \\\\
                        &= 378
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Boxes}                               &:& \text{Biscuits} \\\\
= & 1                                          &:& 54 \\\\
= & 1 \times 7                                 &:& 54 \times 7 \\\\
= & 7                                          &:& 378
\end{array}
$

Total number of biscuits in seven boxes $= 378$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$378$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total biscuits}   &= \text{Number of boxes} \times \text{Number of biscuits in each box} \\\\
                        &= 7 \times 54 \\\\
                        &= 378
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Boxes}                               &:& \text{Biscuits} \\\\
= & 1                                          &:& 54 \\\\
= & 1 \times 7                                 &:& 54 \times 7 \\\\
= & 7                                          &:& 378
\end{array}
$
<emptyline>
Total number of biscuits in seven boxes $= 378$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$378$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-499AEBD4-DCE6-4F71-A016-875B17753D13'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A school has $336$ boys and $485$ girls. How many pupils is this altogether?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total number of pupils}  &= 336 + 485 \\\\
                                &= 821
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$821$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total number of pupils}  &= 336 + 485 \\\\
                                &= 821
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FEEA7D17-856F-460C-80DB-83CFB61B8A34'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

I cook rice for four people. I use $60 \ \text{grams}$ per person. How much, in grammes, remains from a $1 \ \text{kilogram}$ packet?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Rice left}        &= \text{Total rice} - \text{Rice used in cooking} \\\\
                        &= 1 \ \text{kg} - 4 \times 60 \ \text{g} \\\\
                        &= 1000 \ \text{g} - 4 \times 60 \ \text{g} \\\\
                        &= 1000 - 4 \times 60 \\\\
                        &= 1000 - 240 \\\\
                        &= 760 \ \text{g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Person}                              &:& \text{Rice} \\\\
= & 1                                          &:& 60 \\\\
= & 1 \times 4                                 &:& 60 \times 4 \\\\
= & 4                                          &:& 240 \ \text{g}
\end{array}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Rice left}    &= 1 \ \text{kg} - 240 \ \text{g} \\\\
                    &= 1 \times 1000 \ \text{g} - 240 \ \text{g} \\\\
                    &= 1000 - 240 \\\\
                    &= 760 \ \text{g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$760 \ \text{g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Rice left}        &= \text{Total rice} - \text{Rice used in cooking} \\\\
                        &= 1 \ \text{kg} - 4 \times 60 \ \text{g} \\\\
                        &= 1000 \ \text{g} - 4 \times 60 \ \text{g} \\\\
                        &= 1000 - 4 \times 60 \\\\
                        &= 1000 - 240 \\\\
                        &= 760 \ \text{g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
Using ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Person}                              &:& \text{Rice} \\\\
= & 1                                          &:& 60 \\\\
= & 1 \times 4                                 &:& 60 \times 4 \\\\
= & 4                                          &:& 240 \ \text{g}
\end{array}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Rice left}    &= 1 \ \text{kg} - 240 \ \text{g} \\\\
                    &= 1 \times 1000 \ \text{g} - 240 \ \text{g} \\\\
                    &= 1000 - 240 \\\\
                    &= 760 \ \text{g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$760 \ \text{g}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-BE1E370B-4981-405A-9E07-B8FEE8DB45BE'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A giant size toy box contains $400$ bricks. How many bricks will there be in $36$ boxes?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total bricks}     &= \text{Number of boxes} \times \text{Number of bricks in each boxes} \\\\
                        &= 36 \times 400 \\\\
                        &= 14400
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Boxes}                                &:& \text{Bricks} \\\\
= & 1                                           &:& 400 \\\\
= & 1 \times 36                                 &:& 400 \times 36 \\\\
= & 36                                          &:& 14400
\end{array}
$

Number of bricks in $36$ boxes $= 14400$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$14400$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total bricks}     &= \text{Number of boxes} \times \text{Number of bricks in each boxes} \\\\
                        &= 36 \times 400 \\\\
                        &= 14400
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Boxes}                                &:& \text{Bricks} \\\\
= & 1                                           &:& 400 \\\\
= & 1 \times 36                                 &:& 400 \times 36 \\\\
= & 36                                          &:& 14400
\end{array}
$
<emptyline>
Number of bricks in $36$ boxes $= 14400$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$14400$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B7CDC218-814A-4291-B812-C2C813D385FB'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

One inch is about $2.5 \ \text{centimetres}$. How many inches are roughly equal to $1 \ \text{metre}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Value of 1 metre in inches}           &= 1 \ \text{m} \\\\
                                            &= 100 \ \text{cm} \\\\
                                            &= 2.5 \times 40 \\\\
                                            &= 2.5 \ \text{cm} \times 40 \\\\
                                            &= 1 \ \text{inch} \times 40 \\\\
                                            &= 1 \times 40 \\\\
                                            &= 40 \ \text{inches}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$40 \ \text{inches}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Value of 1 metre in inches}           &= 1 \ \text{m} \\\\
                                            &= 100 \ \text{cm} \\\\
                                            &= 2.5 \times 40 \\\\
                                            &= 2.5 \ \text{cm} \times 40 \\\\
                                            &= 1 \ \text{inch} \times 40 \\\\
                                            &= 1 \times 40 \\\\
                                            &= 40 \ \text{inches}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$40 \ \text{inches}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C9858780-383A-4690-AC16-06948BA34755'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

One day in January only $55\%$ of a class of $40$ chidren were present at school.

How many pupils were present?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of pupils present}      &= 55 \% \ \text{of} \ 40 \\\\
                                     &= \dfrac {55} {100} \times 40 \\\\
                                     &= \dfrac {55 \times 40} {100} \\\\
                                     &= \dfrac {5 \times 11 \times 2 \times 20} {5 \times 20} \\\\
                                     &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 11 \times 2 \times \cancel {20} } {\cancel{5} \times \cancel {20}} \\\\
                                     &= 11 \times 2 \\\\
                                     &= 22
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$22$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of pupils present}      &= 55 \% \ \text{of} \ 40 \\\\
                                     &= \dfrac {55} {100} \times 40 \\\\
                                     &= \dfrac {55 \times 40} {100} \\\\
                                     &= \dfrac {5 \times 11 \times 2 \times 20} {5 \times 20} \\\\
                                     &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 11 \times 2 \times \cancel {20} } {\cancel{5} \times \cancel {20}} \\\\
                                     &= 11 \times 2 \\\\
                                     &= 22
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$22$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-8B94F863-C793-41C7-B877-AEAA83C69F16'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The following day $24$ pupils were present, out of $40$. Write this as a percentage.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage of pupils present}     &= \dfrac {24} {40} \times 100 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {24 \times 100} {40} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 20 \times 5} {8 \times 5} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 3 \times 20 \times \cancel {5} } {\cancel {8} \times \cancel {5} } \\\\
                                        &= 3 \times 20 \\\\
                                        &= 60 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$60 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage of pupils present}     &= \dfrac {24} {40} \times 100 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {24 \times 100} {40} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 20 \times 5} {8 \times 5} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 3 \times 20 \times \cancel {5} } {\cancel {8} \times \cancel {5} } \\\\
                                        &= 3 \times 20 \\\\
                                        &= 60 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$60 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-3C95D06E-7409-49E6-A01C-9229FC71E90B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Chocolate bars are packed in boxes of $36$.

A shop buys $14$ boxes.

How many chocolate bars are in $14$ boxes?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total chocolates}     &= \text{Number of boxes} \times \text{Number of chocolates in each box} \\\\    
                            &= 14 \times 36 \\\\
                            &= 504
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Boxes}                                &:& \text{Chocolates} \\\\
= & 1                                           &:& 36 \\\\
= & 1 \times 14                                 &:& 36 \times 14 \\\\
= & 14                                          &:& 504
\end{array}
$

Number of chocolates in $14$ boxes $= 504$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$504$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total chocolates}     &= \text{Number of boxes} \times \text{Number of chocolates in each box} \\\\    
                            &= 14 \times 36 \\\\
                            &= 504
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Boxes}                                &:& \text{Chocolates} \\\\
= & 1                                           &:& 36 \\\\
= & 1 \times 14                                 &:& 36 \times 14 \\\\
= & 14                                          &:& 504
\end{array}
$
<emptyline>
Number of chocolates in $14$ boxes $= 504$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$504$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5CC44C75-9BC0-4F62-9532-420FEB3EF9C8'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A minibus carries $12$ pupils.

How many buses would be needed to take $248$ pupils?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Buses needed}     &= \dfrac {\text{Total pupils}} {\text{Capacity of one bus}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {248} {12} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {62 \times 4} {3 \times 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {62 \times \cancel 4} {3 \times \cancel 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {62} {3} \\\\
                        &= 20 \text{ remainder } 2 \\\\
                        &\approx 21
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$21$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Buses needed}     &= \dfrac {\text{Total pupils}} {\text{Capacity of one bus}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {248} {12} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {62 \times 4} {3 \times 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {62 \times \cancel 4} {3 \times \cancel 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {62} {3} \\\\
                        &= 20 \text{ remainder } 2 \\\\
                        &\approx 21
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$21$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6B95C5CE-23F2-4D24-AD35-37780B943565'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many times can $143$ be subtracted from $855$ before we get to zero?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of times}  &= \dfrac {855} {143} \\\\
                        &= 5 \text{ remainder } 140 \\\\
                        &\approx 5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of times}  &= \dfrac {855} {143} \\\\
                        &= 5 \text{ remainder } 140 \\\\
                        &\approx 5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-63C986B2-7366-43DB-9578-126EFF77F687'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In a bag there are $84$ counters. $\dfrac{5}{7}$ of the counters are blue. How many blue counters are there?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Blue counters}    &= \dfrac {5} {7} \times 84 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 84} {7} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times 7} {7} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
                         &= 5 \times 12 \\\\
                         &= 60
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$60$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Blue counters}    &= \dfrac {5} {7} \times 84 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 84} {7} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times 7} {7} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
                         &= 5 \times 12 \\\\
                         &= 60
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$60$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C9499207-115B-4305-B2DE-454D607FFECD'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$8\%$ of children wear glasses. If there are $1150$ children at a school, how many wear glasses?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Children that wear glasses}   &= 8 \% \ \text{of} \ 1150 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {8} {100} \times 1150 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {8 \times 1150} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 2 \times 2 \times 23 \times 25} {4 \times 25} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 2 \times 2 \times 23 \times \cancel {25} } {\cancel{4} \times \cancel {25}} \\\\
                                    &= 4 \times 23 \\\\
                                    &= 92
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$92$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Children that wear glasses}   &= 8 \% \ \text{of} \ 1150 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {8} {100} \times 1150 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {8 \times 1150} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 2 \times 2 \times 23 \times 25} {4 \times 25} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 2 \times 2 \times 23 \times \cancel {25} } {\cancel{4} \times \cancel {25}} \\\\
                                    &= 4 \times 23 \\\\
                                    &= 92
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$92$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-1B18A3C6-92A4-4CA7-B86D-E416689C9FF1'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A full sweet jar weighs $165.5 \ \text{g}$. The jar contains $90$ sweets. Each sweet weighs $450\ \text{mg}$. How much does the empty jar weigh?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total weight of sweets}     &= 90 \times 450 \ \text {mg} \\\\
                                   &= 90 \times \dfrac {450}{1000} \text {g} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {90 \times 450} {1000} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9 \cancel 0 \times 45 \cancel 0} {10 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {405} {10} \\\\ 
                                   &= 40.5 \ \text {g} \\\\
\text {Weight of empty jar }       &= 162.5 - 40.5 \\\\
                                   &= 122 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$122 \ \text {g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total weight of sweets}     &= 90 \times 450 \ \text {mg} \\\\
                                   &= 90 \times \dfrac {450}{1000} \text {g} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {90 \times 450} {1000} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9 \cancel 0 \times 45 \cancel 0} {10 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {405} {10} \\\\ 
                                   &= 40.5 \ \text {g} \\\\
\text {Weight of empty jar }       &= 162.5 - 40.5 \\\\
                                   &= 122 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$122 \ \text {g}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B1035CFF-9D27-4291-9D78-74E41193350E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A minibus carries $14$ pupils.
How many buses would be needed to take $300$ pupils?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Buses needed}     &= \dfrac {\text{Total pupils}} {\text{Capacity of one bus}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {300} {14} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {150 \times 2} {7 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {150 \times \cancel 2} {7 \times \cancel 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {150} {7} \\\\
                        &= 21 \text{ remainder } 3 \\\\
                        &\approx 22
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$22$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Buses needed}     &= \dfrac {\text{Total pupils}} {\text{Capacity of one bus}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {300} {14} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {150 \times 2} {7 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {150 \times \cancel 2} {7 \times \cancel 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {150} {7} \\\\
                        &= 21 \text{ remainder } 3 \\\\
                        &\approx 22
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$22$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-96E4D14B-256B-459C-BF7E-0AF75C382FD0'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many times can $137$ be subtracted from $1024$ before we get to zero?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of times}  &= \dfrac {1024} {137} \\\\
                        &= 7 \text{ remainder } 65 \\\\
                        &\approx 7
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of times}  &= \dfrac {1024} {137} \\\\
                        &= 7 \text{ remainder } 65 \\\\
                        &\approx 7
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-61A02A7F-E5B3-44BF-9AF9-02A8E1910C57'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In a bag there are $63$ counters. $\dfrac {3} {7}$ of the counters are blue.  How many blue counters are there?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Blue counters}    &= \dfrac {3} {7} \ \text{of} \ 63 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {3} {7} \times 63 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {3 \times 63} {7} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times 7} {7} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
                         &= 3 \times 9 \\\\
                         &= 27
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$27$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Blue counters}    &= \dfrac {3} {7} \ \text{of} \ 63 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {3} {7} \times 63 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {3 \times 63} {7} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times 7} {7} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
                         &= 3 \times 9 \\\\
                         &= 27
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$27$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FE3D8B71-446E-46D9-BCFD-F08E70304636'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$6\%$ of children wear glasses. If there are $1450$ children at a school, how many wear glasses?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Children that wear glasses}   &= 6 \% \ \text{of} \ 1450 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {6} {100} \times 1450 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {6 \times 1450} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 2 \times 29 \times 25} {2 \times 2 \times 25} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 3 \times \cancel {2} \times 29 \times \cancel {25} } {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel {25}} \\\\
                                    &= 3 \times 29 \\\\
                                    &= 87
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$87$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Children that wear glasses}   &= 6 \% \ \text{of} \ 1450 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {6} {100} \times 1450 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {6 \times 1450} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 2 \times 29 \times 25} {2 \times 2 \times 25} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 3 \times \cancel {2} \times 29 \times \cancel {25} } {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel {25}} \\\\
                                    &= 3 \times 29 \\\\
                                    &= 87
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$87$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4682EF96-21BF-4D04-8022-D5229E73F51C'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A full sweet jar weighs $180.5\ \text{g}$. The jar contains $120$ sweets. Each sweet weighs $450\ \text{mg}$.
How much does the empty jar weigh?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total weight of sweets}     &= 120 \times 450 \ \text{mg} \\\\
                                   &= 120 \times \dfrac {450}{1000} \text {g} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {120 \times 450}{1000} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {6 \times 20 \times 9 \times 50}{20 \times 50} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel {20} \times 9 \times \cancel {50}}{\cancel {20} \times \cancel {50}} \\\\
                                   &= 6 \times 9 \\\\
                                   &= 54 \ \text {g} \\\\
\text {Weight of empty jar }       &= 180.5 - 54 \\\\
                                   &= 126.5 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$126.5 \ \text {g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total weight of sweets}     &= 120 \times 450 \ \text{mg} \\\\
                                   &= 120 \times \dfrac {450}{1000} \text {g} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {120 \times 450}{1000} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {6 \times 20 \times 9 \times 50}{20 \times 50} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel {20} \times 9 \times \cancel {50}}{\cancel {20} \times \cancel {50}} \\\\
                                   &= 6 \times 9 \\\\
                                   &= 54 \ \text {g} \\\\
\text {Weight of empty jar }       &= 180.5 - 54 \\\\
                                   &= 126.5 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$126.5 \ \text {g}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

                                &= 495
\end{aligned}

$495$

</div>

$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-CCCD812A-24DA-4BD6-91D0-49B271B1F8BC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A train has $6$ carriages, Each carriage holds $88$ passengers. How many seats has the train have?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total seats in train}     &= \text{Number of carriages} \times \text{Number of seats in each carriage} \\\\
                                &= 6 \times 88 \\\\
                                &= 528
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Carriages}                           &:& \text{Seats} \\\\
= & 1                                          &:& 88 \\\\
= & 1 \times 6                                 &:& 88 \times 6 \\\\
= & 6                                          &:& 528
\end{array}
$

Number of seats in train $= 528$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$528$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total seats in train}     &= \text{Number of carriages} \times \text{Number of seats in each carriage} \\\\
                                &= 6 \times 88 \\\\
                                &= 528
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Carriages}                           &:& \text{Seats} \\\\
= & 1                                          &:& 88 \\\\
= & 1 \times 6                                 &:& 88 \times 6 \\\\
= & 6                                          &:& 528
\end{array}
$
<emptyline>
Number of seats in train $= 528$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$528$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5DA5AA77-9849-4E05-A7A5-DB8108529848'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Two fifths of the pupils in a school wear glasses. What percentage of the pupils wear glasses?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage of pupils with glasses}    &= \dfrac {2} {5} \times 100 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 100} {5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                            &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
                                            &= 40 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$40 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage of pupils with glasses}    &= \dfrac {2} {5} \times 100 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 100} {5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                            &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
                                            &= 40 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$40 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F735C697-C1D6-4F28-881B-25D75A4D04A1'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The UK population is approximately $66{,}482{,}000$. The population of London is $8.78 \ \text{million}$. How many people in the UK live outside London?


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Population outside London}    &= 66482000 - 8.78 \ \text{million} \\\\
                                    &= 66482000 - 8.78 \times 1000000 \\\\
                                    &= 66482000 - 8780000 \\\\
                                    &= 57702000
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$57702000$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Population outside London}    &= 66482000 - 8.78 \ \text{million} \\\\
                                    &= 66482000 - 8.78 \times 1000000 \\\\
                                    &= 66482000 - 8780000 \\\\
                                    &= 57702000
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$57702000$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C354CC85-6043-4133-9B50-EDA130A479B3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many times can $147$ be subtracted from $1270$ before we get to zero?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of times}  &= \dfrac {1270} {147} \\\\
                        &= 8 \text{ remainder } 94 \\\\
                        &\approx 8
\end{aligned}
$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of times}  &= \dfrac {1270} {147} \\\\
                        &= 8 \text{ remainder } 94 \\\\
                        &\approx 8
\end{aligned}
$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-1F8DFCED-55B0-441C-B4D6-0CEFC9911351'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A stationery shop sells $6851$ pens in $31$ days. How many pens per day did they sell?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of pens sold per day}      &= \dfrac {\text{Total pens sold}} {\text{Total number of days}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {6851} {31} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {221 \times 31} {31} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {221 \times \cancel {31}} {\cancel {31}} \\\\
                                        &= 221
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Days}                            &:& \text{Pens} \\\\
= & 31                                     &:& 6851 \\\\
= & 1                                      &:& \dfrac {6851} {31} \\\\
= & 1                                      &:& \dfrac {31 \times 221} {31} \\\\
= & 1                                      &:& \dfrac {\cancel {31} \times 221} {\cancel {31}} \\\\
= & 1                                      &:& 221
\end{array}
$

Number of pens sold by the stationary shop per day $= 221$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$221$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of pens sold per day}      &= \dfrac {\text{Total pens sold}} {\text{Total number of days}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {6851} {31} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {221 \times 31} {31} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {221 \times \cancel {31}} {\cancel {31}} \\\\
                                        &= 221
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{Days}                            &:& \text{Pens} \\\\
= & 31                                     &:& 6851 \\\\
= & 1                                      &:& \dfrac {6851} {31} \\\\
= & 1                                      &:& \dfrac {31 \times 221} {31} \\\\
= & 1                                      &:& \dfrac {\cancel {31} \times 221} {\cancel {31}} \\\\
= & 1                                      &:& 221
\end{array}
$
<emptyline>
Number of pens sold by the stationary shop per day $= 221$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$221$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B49A2D25-54CB-4ACB-A269-1B71CCD90AA4'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A medicine bottle contains $90$ pills. Each pill weighs $250 \  \text{mg}$, The full bottle weighs $160 \  \text{g}$. How much does the empty bottle weigh?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total weight of pills}      &= 90 \times 250 \ \text{mg} \\\\
                                   &= 90 \times \dfrac {250} {1000} \ \text {g} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {90 \times 250} {1000} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9 \cancel 0 \times 25 \cancel 0} {10 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times 25} {10} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {225} {10} \\\\
                                   &= 22.5 \ \text {g} \\\\
\text {Weight of empty bottle}     &= 160 - 22.5 \\\\
                                   &= 137.5 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$137.5 \ \text {g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total weight of pills}      &= 90 \times 250 \ \text{mg} \\\\
                                   &= 90 \times \dfrac {250} {1000} \ \text {g} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {90 \times 250} {1000} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9 \cancel 0 \times 25 \cancel 0} {10 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times 25} {10} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {225} {10} \\\\
                                   &= 22.5 \ \text {g} \\\\
\text {Weight of empty bottle}     &= 160 - 22.5 \\\\
                                   &= 137.5 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$137.5 \ \text {g}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-576FEBF3-4204-4EE6-B775-864A333384B2'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In a school $24\%$ of children have blond hair. If there are $1100$ children at a school, how many have blond hair?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Children that have blond hair}   &= 24 \% \ \text{of} \ 1100 \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24} {100} \times 1100 \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24 \times 1100} {100} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24 \times 11 \cancel {00} } {1 \cancel {00} } \\\\
                                       &= 24 \times 11 \\\\
                                       &= 264
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$264$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Children that have blond hair}   &= 24 \% \ \text{of} \ 1100 \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24} {100} \times 1100 \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24 \times 1100} {100} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac {24 \times 11 \cancel {00} } {1 \cancel {00} } \\\\
                                       &= 24 \times 11 \\\\
                                       &= 264
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$264$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C04EC8BD-232A-4534-82B7-F01510B9A4FD'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Chocolate bars are packed in boxes of $12$. A shop buys $16$ boxes.

How many chocolate bars are in $16$ boxes?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total chocolate bars} &= 12 \times 16 \\\\
                            &= 192
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$192$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total chocolate bars} &= 12 \times 16 \\\\
                            &= 192
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-9C590E47-8109-44CC-B4E1-697A79F9B187'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A minibus carries $16$ pupils.

How many buses would be needed to take $300$ pupils?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of bused required} &= \dfrac {300} {16} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4 \times 75} {4 \times 4} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times 75} {\cancel 4 \times 4} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75} {4} \\\\
                                &= 18 \text{ remainder } 3 \\\\
                                &\approx 19
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$19$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of bused required} &= \dfrac {300} {16} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4 \times 75} {4 \times 4} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times 75} {\cancel 4 \times 4} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75} {4} \\\\
                                &= 18 \text{ remainder } 3 \\\\
                                &\approx 19
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-AB82D7BB-6F34-4053-AC0A-D359B89A2F54'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many times can $117$ be subtracted from $1200$ before we get to zero?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of times} &= \dfrac {1200} {117} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {3 \times 400} {3 \times 39} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 400} {\cancel 3 \times 39} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {400} {39} \\\\
                       &= 10 \text{ remainder } 10 \\\\
                       &\approx 10
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$10$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of times} &= \dfrac {1200} {117} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {3 \times 400} {3 \times 39} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 400} {\cancel 3 \times 39} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {400} {39} \\\\
                       &= 10 \text{ remainder } 10 \\\\
                       &\approx 10
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$10$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4E4B9BEE-6BB1-40F9-851E-3B5493022051'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In a bag there are $63$ counters.$\dfrac {5} {9}$ of the counters are blue. How many blue counters are there?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Blue counters}    &= \dfrac {5} {9} \ \text {of} \ 63 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5} {9} \times 63 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 63} {9} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 7 \times 9} {9} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 7 \times \cancel {9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
                         &= 5 \times 7 \\\\
                         &= 35
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$35$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Blue counters}    &= \dfrac {5} {9} \ \text {of} \ 63 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5} {9} \times 63 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 63} {9} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 7 \times 9} {9} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 7 \times \cancel {9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
                         &= 5 \times 7 \\\\
                         &= 35
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$35$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-227E8AEF-0B10-412A-8725-7A4D3CA82AE9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$6 \%$ of children wear glasses. If there are $1450$ children at a school, how many wear glasses?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Children that wear glasses}   &= 6 \% \ \text{of} \ 1450 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {6} {100} \times 1450 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {6 \times 1450} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 2 \times 29 \times 25} {2 \times 2 \times 25} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 3 \times \cancel {2} \times 29 \times \cancel {25} } {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel {25}} \\\\
                                    &= 3 \times 29 \\\\
                                    &= 87
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$87$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Children that wear glasses}   &= 6 \% \ \text{of} \ 1450 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {6} {100} \times 1450 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {6 \times 1450} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 2 \times 29 \times 25} {2 \times 2 \times 25} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 3 \times \cancel {2} \times 29 \times \cancel {25} } {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel {25}} \\\\
                                    &= 3 \times 29 \\\\
                                    &= 87
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$87$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F9E14616-3855-4C02-8422-69B65279E5DB'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A full sweet jar weighs $180.5 \ \text{g}$. The jar contains $120$ sweets. Each sweet weighs $450 \ \text{mg}$.
How much does the empty jar weigh?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total weight of sweets}     &= 120 \times 450 \ \text{mg} \\\\
                                   &= 120 \times \dfrac {450}{1000} \ \text {g} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {120 \times 450} {1000} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {6 \times 20 \times 9 \times 50} {20 \times 50} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel {20} \times 9 \times \cancel {50}} {\cancel {20} \times \cancel {50}} \\\\
                                   &= 6 \times 9 \\\\
                                   &= 54 \ \text {g} \\\\
\text {Weight of empty jar }       &= 180.5 - 54 \\\\
                                   &= 126.5 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$126.5 \ \text {g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total weight of sweets}     &= 120 \times 450 \ \text{mg} \\\\
                                   &= 120 \times \dfrac {450}{1000} \ \text {g} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {120 \times 450} {1000} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {6 \times 20 \times 9 \times 50} {20 \times 50} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel {20} \times 9 \times \cancel {50}} {\cancel {20} \times \cancel {50}} \\\\
                                   &= 6 \times 9 \\\\
                                   &= 54 \ \text {g} \\\\
\text {Weight of empty jar }       &= 180.5 - 54 \\\\
                                   &= 126.5 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-553D9630-2488-43F6-B40B-249573F31F80'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many $15\ \text{cm}$ strips can I cut from a ribbon $1\ \text{m}$ long?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of strips}    &= \dfrac {1 \ \text {m}} {15 \ \text {cm}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1 \times 100 \ \text {cm}} {15 \ \text {cm}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times 20} {5 \times 3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 20} {\cancel 5 \times 3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {20} {3} \\\\
                            &= 6 \text { remainder } 2 \\\\
                            &\approx 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of strips}    &= \dfrac {1 \ \text {m}} {15 \ \text {cm}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1 \times 100 \ \text {cm}} {15 \ \text {cm}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times 20} {5 \times 3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 20} {\cancel 5 \times 3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {20} {3} \\\\
                            &= 6 \text { remainder } 2 \\\\
                            &\approx 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-CABD97FF-A137-4136-B3F8-465E5FFFF48B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A box contains $48$ pencils.

If I take out $\dfrac {3} {4}$ of the pencils how many would be left?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Pencils left}     &= \left( 1 - \dfrac {3} {4} \right) \times 48 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 48 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {48} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {12 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
                        &= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Pencils left}     &= \left( 1 - \dfrac {3} {4} \right) \times 48 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 48 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {48} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {12 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
                        &= 12
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$12$
